2026-11-05 03:47:20.093 |           Alternatively, you can also rely on setuptools' discovery methods
2026-11-05 03:47:20.093 |           (for example by using `find_namespace_packages(...)`/`find_namespace:`
2026-11-05 03:47:20.093 |           instead of `find_packages(...)`/`find:`).

2026-11-05 03:47:20.093 |   
2026-11-05 03:47:20.093 |           - https://setuptools.pypa.io/en/latest/userguide/package_discovery.html
